This file is the project's further research and holds nothing closed: every open thing by name, for a researcher to take, each with what nature shows, what the law has in its status words, what is missing, where to start and, where a local study exists, what was checked locally on 2026-10-17 with its numbers, and what 3.0 built on 2026-10-19 as a test, named "built on 2026-10-19" with its test file, and what 2026-10-20 built after the tag, named "built on 2026-10-20" with its law paragraph (the day's three pull requests: the click that runs back with the write at the record's birth and the cone's run the board's run exactly, the exclusion from the family's sign, the atom folder with the hydrogen worlds laid from the Node and the law's file matched to the measurements, the fine structure Klein-Gordon's, the constants round, the click's read of the board with the totals' bias and what a body reads, and the toy atom worlds in the exact vacuum deleted on the owner's word). What the port of 2026-10-21 entered as declared layers of the files (pull request #2166: the Lorentz form on the sign holder's four lines, the shear lines of a form-sourced row, the carried uniform mode of a closed board, the contact holder as a frozen row) is named "the port of 2026-10-21" with its law paragraph, and what the build it ports left out or open (the covector act, the mixed act, the conversion's axis filter and the contact read, the far-zone proof of the Lorentz form with the sourcing's finding; the start's booking kick, found and corrected the same day, stands in the law's start paragraph, and the odd line's read sign, decided by the law's own (b2) check the same day it was found, in the law's lines) stands here by name, dated 2026-10-21. What the builder's round of 2026-10-21 (pull request #2185, the branch claude/sprint-takers-pixel-conversion) built, measured and did not close stands here by name with its measurement and its direction, dated 2026-10-21, the builder's round, nothing of it in the law as a decided line and nothing of it in src. What the law has closed stands as its bold lines; a part closed on 2026-10-17 or on 2026-10-19 is named here by its law line, the open remainder kept; a number of 2026-10-19 said to be derived is derived from the files and the law with no run behind it (the owner's standing word of 2026-10-19: only code, without worlds and without experiments, only tests; a clean and generic engine). The words are the law's, defined in the README's [The words, from the law](../README.md#the-words-from-the-law); a pointer is a bold line of [the law](ALGEBRA.md) with its section on first use, "item N" an item of [what is open](ALGEBRA.md#what-is-open), B.9 the claims list of Online Resource 1. The status words are the law's with two of this file's. "Checked locally, no world" is a derivation or run outside the repository, on the law's line or on a local variant (a plain copy of main with named functions or lines replaced, never the engine), with no world of the repository; the engine's own clicks on a locally composed world are named as the engine's, and each check names its instrument. "A declaration, not made" is a line the owner could add, nothing derived.

The former variants left on that word and their readings stand in the folder's notes as dated paragraphs read on the former variants; the items below name them where a reading rests on one.

- **The contraction read as acceleration by a one-way clock, on single clicks.** The one world's 22 clicks by stage: 11 through the deceleration (3.4 per 100 intervals), 1 at the return, 8 through the second rise (1.3 per 100), 2 through the second contraction (0.6 per 100); the light's quanta at the instrument's Node grow through the turned stretch and the records run one way, and the first contraction holds one click, so the reading of a rate through it rests on single clicks and no more. - **The structured course's end: the tear at the second rise** (2026-10-23; the bijection hand's and the clicks hand's readings on the former two-instrument variant and the small structured world; ALGEBRA.md, The carried momentum, The swap at the top, The stress is the Links'). (a) The cut at 1,192 read: the Link (2, 4, 0)-(2, 0, 0) across the wrap and its mirror by the board's symmetry, four Links at the tension 1,002 against Gamma 1,000, a mixed Link, one end fired three times and time-reversed, rising to its own top at 1,184, the other fired twice and falling from its top at 987; the two time lines crossing near 1,115 and diverging by about 22,000 per interval, the Link's tension rising from 702 at 1,140 to 1,002 at 1,192's read, fed by the records' squared differences across the Link (matter and light at the two ends); the stress reads a plain line's levels as the state holds them, the law's sentence that its neighbours read its levels as they stand, so nothing is unframed and no fix is owed: the tear is the course's own. (b) The scaling: the small structured world run to its refusal at Gamma 1,000, 2,000 and 4,000 with every integer scaled by the one factor (the gap, the pairs, the clump's levels, the light's level and velocity, T and the counts; the width and the Link unit unscaled) runs the same course to the third decimal of U, the first top at 34, the volume's top at 40 (U 0.9028, 0.9027, 0.9026), the fires at the same intervals, the trough at 75 and the refusal at 78 at every scale, 2.29 tops at all three Gammas; on the 3-cube the refusal is the crunch (X at most 0 at a Node) and on the 5-cube the Link's tension at the wall, two faces of one end; so Gamma is not the room, and the tear is the structured course's own (scaling T by the factor's square instead is another universe, its wall growing as the cube of the factor, fired every few intervals and refused by the tension past the wall). (c) What to research: the law's sentence at the course's two ends, X at 0, the singular point, and a Link's tension at the wall, to be derived and approved before entering; endless bangs on a structured universe are not the law's as written. The owner's reading (2026-10-23, 12:5x UTC), a candidate and not the law: a Link whose two ends' time lines differ by more than the carrier holds in one step is a place where the two sides can no longer read each other, a horizon; the Node whose X reaches 0 is the singular point inside it; the tear at a mixed Link, a time-reversed region beside a forward one, is the place where one side's time stands against the other's; the candidate sentence: at the wall the Link stops reading, the two sides decouple and each side's course goes on, instead of the run refusing, so a horizon forms inside the course and the universe outside goes on; to be derived and approved before entering. The derivation delivered the same day (the bijection hand, on the owner's order, not in this landing): the horizon as the Link's factor held at the wall, q = max(Gamma - t, 0), a function of the state with no act and no log, the Link re-coupling when the tension falls, the run back exact by the cut Link's identity; what remains before it enters: the frozen Node (both Links at the wall, p_a = 0) and the plain current reading through a decoupled Link, to derive first; and the reading that the wall is not always reached before X = 0 (the small structured board's crunch at 78 with the largest tension 440 against 1,000), so the singular point can stand outside any horizon. Closed 2026-10-23 to **The horizon** (ALGEBRA.md, the owner's word: the tear is a horizon, let the run continue): the Link at the wall is read at the wall, its factor 0, the run going on with the two ends decoupled and re-coupling when the tension falls, the run back exact through it, nothing lost; the singular point outside a horizon (the universe test's small board reached X at most 0 at 78 with the largest Link tension 440 against 1,000; every uniform board's tensions vanish) closed the same day into **The floor** (ALGEBRA.md): the Node turns at the least volume with a reading and no course reaches X = 0, the small board running on past 78 with its run back exact; the one world's course past its former cut read in the horizon's round.
- **The one world's end at 3,968 is the click's, not the course's** (2026-10-23, read on the one world run on under **The horizon** with the run parameter 4,000 and again toward 10,000, the same interval both times, on the branch's course at the documents head b8d2f2ce6 before the landing's merges; the shipped run stops at 1,500). With the Links' refusal gone the Node's law refuses a run at two places alone, the volume at most 0 at a Node and the amplitude bound on a line's level, and neither was reached: the least X on the board over the run 50,712 at 1,400, the board's mean volume topping at 957, 1,843 and 2,829, each higher than the one before, the horizon forming at 1,304, dissolving at 1,436 and re-forming on every later contraction, up to 204 Links at the wall at once at 3,225, the largest tension 3,886 at 3,755, the fifth act firing at 573 intervals, the instruments clicking on behind and beside the horizon (the click written on the lattice 182 times by 1,474 against the shipped run's 159 to 1,304, the births to 1,410 and after). The run ended at 3,968 by the click's read of the board at the absorber's draw (**The click's read of the board**, digits.read_of): the draw's total 100 is the product of its gcd 100 with the read's bases less one and its gcd 1 with its last base, so the draw is the digits' sum modulo the total alone, no uniform part left to place, and the draw is refused by name. This is the click's own letter and refuses the click, not the course: the course to that interval stands. Open: whether the read's bases at a late interval of a long run always reach this degeneracy (the digits' lattice F growing with the records' levels, the bases w div F + 1 shrinking toward the total's factors), and what the law's sentence should be at a draw that carries no uniform part (the click withheld and the course going on, or the draw taken on the digits' sum alone), for the clicks hand; until it is written, a long run of the one world ends at the first such draw, and the run parameter of the shipped logs is set below it.
- **Matter's birth from light: the meeting of two quanta at one Node** (2026-10-23, a law sentence CANDIDATE and not the law, from the owner's question of that day, when nature gives birth to matter, that is when it happens here). The reading in the law as written: light has no gap and converts into nothing by the lists derived from the pairs; the law's creation of matter is a parent's conversion, one click taking a quantum of a parent and giving quanta of its children (the conversion worlds, examples/events/conversion/); the undepleted beam's sentence (ALGEBRA.md, The click writes on the lattice (b) and (d)) books a second quantum arriving at a Node whose share stands at a quantum as the books' deficit and writes it nowhere (read on the former birth variant's small board, tests/test_the_sign_holder_and_the_pairs_on_the_small_torus.py's clause (4): the light's deficit 7 at seven closes; the conversion chains' deficits 1 at their one close). In nature that meeting in the hot start, where a quantum carries more than a pair's rest energy, makes the pair, and the production stops below the threshold. What to research: whether a sentence of the law turns that booked meeting, two quanta at one Node, into a gapped pair's record at the Node in the first intervals, where the time line is short, the clock fast and the light still far from uniform, derived from the pairs with no key and with the threshold as the pairs give it; the owner's approval first, the sentence entered under its own name before any code. Derived and found not to hold under The conversion as written (the universe hand, 2026-10-23, on the owner's word to derive it first; tests/test_two_light_quanta_make_no_pair.py): the click's conservation is the rotation's exactness, the parents' Gaussian product over their dens equal to the children's, the senses entering the charge's sum alone and never the point; two gapless quanta have the point (den, 0) each and the identity as their product, a list completing it needs the children's product real and positive, and no pair of this project's universes gives it: two records of one gapped pair give (num + i s)^2, imaginary where num is above 0 and -s^2 below 0 at num 0 (two protons at [0, 1000]: -1,000,000), two records of different pairs a point off the real axis (600 + 800 i times 800 + 600 i = 1,000,000 i); the law's own lister derives no list for two light quanta in the three Universe24 universes at any size it searches (every list of at most N + BEYOND records, the count of a birth the irreducible list's and not two by decree, two children where the levels carry one gap and more where they carry more, as the owner asked), so the second quantum at a Node stays the books' deficit, as The click writes on the lattice reads it. What would hold, named and not entered: a conservation in which the sense conjugates the point, a record at the sense -1 the point num - i s, under which every plane pair at opposite senses completes two light quanta exactly ((num + i s)(num - i s) = den^2) with the threshold the levels' energy against the pair's two walls, 2 x 3 T sqrt(den^2 - num^2) at or below 2 x 3 den T (the electron pair of the matter universe at 3,600 T against 6,000 T, the proton pair at 6,000 T exactly); the law's text has the sense in the charge's sum and not in the point, and the owner's decision on that sentence is the next step. ENTERED as the gapless meeting's own clause on the owner's word of 2026-10-23 ("if that is how nature looks, put it in"; the universe hand's build on the branch matter-birth; ALGEBRA.md, Matter's birth from light): The conversion's exactness and its charge convention stay as written for every parent (the sense the charge's sign, the point the same at both senses, the neutron's chain standing on it, no shipped list changed, the 27 universes asserted by name in tests/test_two_light_quanta_make_no_pair.py); two quanta of a gapless family meeting at a Node, whose parents' own list is empty, make the conjugate pair of a plane family (`conversion.birth_of`: every plane family with a gap and a Pythagorean pair at the senses -1 and 1, the lightest first; the pair's product the identity, the charge kept by the senses' sum 0; the threshold the two quanta's two walls against the pair's two walls), laid at the Node in the click's write path where the deficit was booked (`act.born_pair`, two items of the same step, the births' book, the lay logged and undone on the run back). Read on the small structured board (tests/test_matter_is_born_from_light.py): the absorber's emitted light quantum meeting the uniform light's at [2, 2, 2] at the interval 45 births the electron pair at the senses -1 and 1, the electron's record standing there from nothing, the run back exact 70 of 70 through the birth. Open by name: the books' reading of a conjugate pair at one Node (the pair's share reads one quantum against the books' two, the electron's deficit -1 as read) and the take's own deficit at the absorber's Node (the undepleted beam at the absorption, 1, standing as before); a birth at every Node by the rule alone, with no draw, a further sentence not entered; on the one world (universe24.json) eight electron pairs born at the two instruments' Nodes at their emissions (85, 150, 180, 210, 300, 465, 540, 1,125), the electron's record at [0, 0, 0] standing from nothing to 26,603 at 540 and 7,186 at the cut; read on the former matter-birth variant, two births at 75 and 270. Open by name: a birth at a Node with no instrument, the two quanta of the standing light alone, which the run shows nowhere (every birth at an emission into the standing light), and whether the threshold or the draw's write path keeps it so.
